41: ## §C3-v2 — registry back-references (calibration), re-registered
42: 
43: **Why the label changed.** The measured quantity is registry back-reference consistency between entry text and `used-by`
44: cells (token level), reviewed for name-only mentions. It is not a measure of how well the lane calibrates its confidence;
45: the SCORECARD row carries that wording. The axis stays calibration because the lane's own registry (its statement of what it
46: relies on) is compared with the repository state.
47: 
48: **Definitions** (at `--rev` unless named; ENTRIES, ROWS, CITE, USEDBY, SRC as in registration.md §C3):
49: - PAIRS(rev) = {(S, entry) : S is a registry row id at rev, entry in ENTRIES(rev), S in CITE(entry)}.
50: - `c3-name-only.tsv` (frozen, sha256 `c3.allowlist_sha256` in `frozen-tables-v2.json`; path `.ufo/waves/round-56/c3-name-only.tsv`): 24 tab-separated rows `S###`, entry path, line-sha, reason. They are the pairs that AT LEAST ONE of two independent wave-1 readers judged name-only (R56RevC3Contract `MIS` or R56CritCiteDef `N`/`R`/`M-N`): the entry mentions the id to refute, supersede, redirect, disclaim or name another object, so adding the entry to that row's `used-by` would record reliance the text denies. LINESHA(S, entry, rev) = sha256 over the UTF-8 bytes of the lines of the entry at `rev` that are outside fenced code blocks and contain the token S (token rule of CITE), in file order, each line as in the blob split on `\n` (no stripping), joined by `\n`.
51: - `missing` = |{(S, entry) in PAIRS(rev) : entry not in USEDBY(S) and (S, entry) not an allowlist row}|.
52: - `extra` = |{(S, path) : path in USEDBY(S) and (path is not an ENTRIES member or S not in CITE(path))}| (unchanged).
53: - `dangling` = |{(entry, S) : S in SRC(entry), S not a registry row id}| (unchanged).
54: - `conflict` = |{allowlist rows (S, entry) with entry in USEDBY(S)}|.
55: - `stale` = |{allowlist rows whose entry is absent at rev, or whose S is not in CITE(entry), or whose LINESHA(S, entry, rev) differs from the row's line-sha}|.
56: - `lost` = |{(S, entry) in PAIRS(base) : (S, entry) not in PAIRS(rev)}| where PAIRS(base) is computed from the lane objects at the frozen `base` revision (`frozen-tables-v2.json` `c3.base_rev`; its sorted `S###|path` lines joined by `\n` plus a final `\n` have sha256 `c3.base_pairs_sha256` — the counter fails closed when the objects are absent or the hash differs).
57: - `allow_sha`: the blob `c3-name-only.tsv` at `--rev` has sha256 `c3.allowlist_sha256` (a miss prints `missed=allow_sha`).
58: - `exempt` = the number of allowlist rows (frozen: 24).
59: Output: `MADO_USED_BY: rev=<rev> entries=<E> rows=<R> missing=<m> extra=<x> dangling=<d> conflict=<c> stale=<s> lost=<l> exempt=<n> verdict=<TARGET_MET|TARGET_MISSED> missed=<legs,comma|none>`; `TARGET_MET` only at `m=x=d=c=s=l=0`, `n=24` and every of `allow_sha`, `tables_sha` ok; exit 0/1/2. Baseline at the base revision (`receipts/c3-base-v2.txt`): `entries=70 rows=319 missing=41 extra=0 dangling=0 conflict=0 stale=0 lost=0 exempt=24`.
60: 
61: **Harness (`--claim c3`) — 9 controls**, one mutation each on a scratch commit of the good tree at `--rev`; the counter must exit 1 with the required leg in `missed`; line `MADO_R56_CONTROLS: claim=c3 good=<ok|bad> controls=<k>/9 verdict=<OK|FAIL>`:
62: 1. `base-registry` — `corpus/SOURCES.md` replaced by its bytes at the frozen base → `missing`.
63: 2. `fill-all` — every row's used-by gets every entry path → `extra`.
64: 3. `drop-one` — remove the first entry (sorted) that is not an allowlist pair for the first row (sorted by id) that lists it, from that row's used-by → `missing`.
65: 4. `dangling-source` — append ` S999` to the `source:` line of the first entry (sorted) that has one → `dangling`.
66: 5. `ghost-path` — add `corpus/knowledge/no-such-entry.md` to the used-by of S001 → `extra`.
67: 6. `fenced-cite` — wrap in a ``` fence the line holding the first non-allowlist, single-occurrence citation (first by sorted (S, entry)) while used-by still lists it → `extra` (and `lost`).
68: 7. `allow-everything` — replace `c3-name-only.tsv` by a file listing every pair of PAIRS(rev) with correct line-shas → `allow_sha`.
69: 8. `delete-citation` — in the first entry (sorted) that has a base pair outside the allowlist, replace one occurrence of its S-token on a citing line by `S---` while used-by is untouched → `lost` (collateral `extra`, `missing` allowed).
70: 9. `stale-allow` — append ` x` to the first line (file order) of the first allowlist row's LINESHA lines → `stale`.

80: ## What v2 does not repair (declared limits, carried into the report)
81: 
82: C1: `TARGET_MET` proves quote identity, registry binding, anchor scope and lexical grounding; a forged proposition that shares a
83: quote identifier, keeps to the quote's numbers and does not echo the quote passes (R56RevC1Contract §2); entailment is the wave-2
84: sentence-to-pin audit (reported, not counted) and the non-author closure. C2: a tool that fingerprints the tree and replays the
85: committed table scores 18/18 committed and fails the hold-out only (the hold-out is the guard); a tool that reads the lane's
86: revealed hold-out by absolute path passes (non-adversarial harness). C3: `CITE` reads literal tokens only (ranges such as
87: `S175–S177` hide `S176`, five pairs; entries without a `source:` line are invisible to `dangling`, 20 entries); the
88: allowlist is two readers' judgment, frozen, disclosed and open to the non-author closure; the 41 pairs that remain are filled and were judged
89: reliance (or leaning reliance) by BOTH readers (R56RevC3Contract `REAL`/`BORD`, R56CritCiteDef `U`/`M-U`); six carry an `M-U` or `BORD` hedge.
